Dismissing the first advances the saved queue but leaves the second toast hidden until reload. Both independent browser probes reproduce it. Next loop: notification identity/timer correction and TypeScript view migration, then exact retests. The adjacent repeated Hall seasonal-reset path on flip dismissal is separately pending inspection.
- `node --experimental-strip-types artifacts/v2/synergy-resume/simulate.mjs`: 100 recorded seeded reducer campaigns, 52 victories/48 losses, 2,054 accepted management actions, zero save failures. Assertions cover input immutability, deterministic repeat transitions, RNG/range bounds, unique building IDs, non-stalling phases, and save roundtrips. This is headless reducer evidence, not natural browser campaign evidence. Compared with the historical pre-correction 55/45 run, five former victories now end in famine, two former losses now win, and fifteen losses change turn. `campaign-comparison.json` records all 22 differences; this comparison alone does not isolate each correction's causal effect.
- `node artifacts/v2/synergy-resume/code-census.mjs`: same physical-line categories against original baseline. Implementation 30,642 -> 31,673; authored data 13,566 -> 13,853; styles 1,139 -> 1,164; executable tests 3,585 -> 7,153; selected tooling 1,879 -> 1,893; documentation 3,089 -> 2,921; other text 8,382 -> 9,455. Production totals **45,347 -> 46,690 (+1,343)**; inclusive text 62,282 -> 68,112 before this evidence update. Mixed TypeScript remains counted as implementation/data. Production reduction remains unmet.

All evidence above is scoped to the recorded build. Full migration, final interface coverage, full current browser/campaign acceptance, and final independent verdict remain open.

## Baseline and broad suite

Baseline `npm ci`, `npm run build`, and `npm run lint` passed; `npx playwright test --list` discovered 109 tests in 16 files. A reliable full baseline browser suite was not run because the old config could reuse an unrelated server on port 5173. The candidate `playwright.config.ts` starts its own strict server on 127.0.0.1:5182 with no reuse.

An earlier candidate full `npx playwright test --workers=1 --reporter=line` run discovered 117 tests: 103 passed, 13 failed, 1 skipped, in 20.6 minutes. All 13 failures were missing `chromium-win32` screenshot goldens; tracked goldens are `chromium-linux`. Generated Windows images are retained only in ignored `artifacts/v2/full-suite-windows-golden-candidates/` pending visual review. This full suite predates the latest save, selector, and navigation changes and has not been rerun at the current fingerprint. The six legacy helper profiles from that run are in the earlier record; they were unseeded and not then asserted.

Tester T-EENG01 used +100 to suppress output in a 390px Market/season path; grader G-EENG-01 used -100 to create free space in a 300/300 store, yielding 5 coal, 3 leather, and 3 wood at 390/1366px. The archived engine has the same numeric behavior; the defect belongs to the save boundary. The grader's separate 1,200-case simulation/16,800-helper comparison found zero output/report/draw differences. Red unit and mobile browser cases reproduced the accepted unknown key. Save import/write now checks each inventory key against `RESOURCE_CONFIG`, retaining the 17 required and three optional authored quantities; the `Inventory` type moved from save to `data/economy.ts`. The exact red cases pass. Current local checks: typecheck/lint/build, 89/89 units, 57/57 adjacent browsers on an isolated strict server with zero retries, 180-case parity, and 100 campaigns (55/45/zero save failures). The original reviewer browser probes need exact retest; reports: `artifacts/v2/{tester,grader}-economy-engine-ts-review/`.
- Exact economy retest at frozen `9BE56B6E…`: tester's original +100 phantom item and -100 variant reject visibly on 390px v2 Load and legacy Import; grader reproduced -100/+100 rejection at 390/1366px. Both confirmed unchanged original bytes, no v2 slot from a failed legacy import, and a playable title. Valid older and optional-provision imports, legal production, full-store season progression, and a pending choice/save continued. Tester ran 19/19 strict focused browsers, 89/89 units, and typecheck; grader ran 19/19 browsers, 20/20 save units, typecheck, and archived 180-case parity. Neither found a new scoped P0–P3. Opened full-size mobile alert and desktop legal-choice screenshots; reports: `artifacts/v2/{tester,grader}-economy-engine-ts-retest/`. This accepts the scoped P2 repair, not full nested-save or whole-game 2.0 completion.

## Independent findings and visual evidence

The tester and grader found and retested malformed-save acceptance (T-B01), fabricated pending events (G-S01), offscreen error feedback (G-S02), corrupted known-event effects (G-R2-01), missing consequence names (G-R2-02), mobile first-viewport crowding (G-R2-03), missing flip/raid phase payloads (T-R3-01), altered finite authored event effects (T-R3-02), and legitimate fractional raid result rejection (G-R4-01). Exact reproductions, evidence, and scoped verdicts are under `artifacts/v2/tester-*` and `artifacts/v2/grader-*`. The grader independently confirmed G-R4-01 fixed in `grader-retest-5`: accepted warning, reducer-produced result, legacy read, v2 write/read; tested ratio bounds. No unresolved P0/P1 is known in these targeted paths. Comprehensive nested save shapes and every phase/branch remain unverified.

The tester and grader found T-F01 (transparent raid result panel), T-F02 (zero-garrison message incorrectly credits soldiers), and T-F03 (stale contributor guidance), all P2 at fingerprint `DD187AB7…`. The panel now uses an opaque gradient and the message credits fortifications. Both reviewers independently retested T-F01/T-F02 at `35D5B555…` and accepted the opened full-size screenshots and browser continuation. They found remaining documentation claims about 5173, outdated 25/50 raid thresholds, and automatic zero-garrison loss; CLAUDE/README now state the actual 5180 worktree port, 18/38 thresholds, and possible fortified zero-garrison victory. The later scoped grader review accepted these rule corrections. Evidence: `artifacts/v2/raid-result-after/`, `tester-p2-retest/`, `grader-p2-retest/`.

At saved-PRNG fingerprint `A7BC9A72…`, the independent tester found that Horizon Scan rendered no anomalies (T-RNG-01, P1) and replayed different ambient-random scans (T-RNG-02, P2). The grader reproduced those defects and found reducer-produced terminal states rejected by the save validator (G-RNG-02, P2), fabricated/repeated scan rewards (G-RNG-04, P2), unreachable Eagle-eyed rating (G-RNG-05, P2), and stale fixed-draw parity evidence (G-RNG-06, P3). The primary repaired these with a typed seeded planner/scorer, canonical reducer rewards and guards, reachable all-five rating, and structured terminal reason validation. Both reviewers independently accepted those original findings as fixed in tested paths at `DA8EFCB6…`; see `artifacts/v2/tester-random-state-retest/` and `grader-random-state-retest/`. They found two new P2s: overlapping 32px scan targets for a valid seed, and a five-find acknowledgement button clipped by 20px in the first 1366×768 viewport. The primary placed anomalies in five shuffled horizontal lanes and put long observations in a labeled scroll region while keeping the reward and action visible. At `D22069EE…`, both reviewers independently retested the exact overlap seed and five-find viewport: normal Dust/Birds pointer clicks succeeded, the 40px action occupied y674.8–714.8 within 768px, and all five findings remained reachable by inner scroll. No new P1/P2 in these scoped paths. See `artifacts/v2/tester-watchtower-layout-retest/`, `grader-watchtower-layout-retest/`, and opened primary captures in `watchtower-final/`. The grader inspected but did not drive the 1280×720 and 390×844 report captures.

At first initial-state fingerprint `C62B09E4…`, the tester and grader found that family bonus objects remained shared across games, and the grader found injected market draws accepted NaN/out-of-range values. Exact probes are in `artifacts/v2/tester-initial-state/` and `grader-initial-state/`. The primary now clones each nested family bonus and rejects draws outside finite `[0, 1)`; new unit regressions and all static/focused checks pass. Both reviewers independently confirmed their exact reproductions fixed at fingerprint `130C0DB6…`; reports are in `tester-initial-state-retest/` and `grader-initial-state-retest/`. The tester's browser replay evidence applies to the earlier parity-equivalent initial-state fingerprint; the final guarded-draw and clone corrections had unit and independent probe coverage, not a repeated browser campaign.

The primary's Gambit red regression showed that the old `TAVERN_GAMBIT_RESULT` command could credit a forged 1,000,000 denarii win. The new reducer-owned seeded command derives the opponent, outcome, earnings, and next RNG state after validating phase, choice, wager, seed, funds, and five-round limit. Four unit tests and the original two browser tests passed. Opened `artifacts/v2/gambit-browser/` screenshots show the first result at 1366×768 and choice/result at 390×844 after correcting the displayed round and card width. At exact fingerprint `74F65B5B…`, the tester and grader independently accepted sampled reducer guards/rule parity but both found a fourth-round continuation defect; both reproduced a pending wager settling after Load during the reveal. The tester ranked Load as P1 because the just-restored state changes, while the grader ranked it P2. Reports and screenshots are in `artifacts/v2/tester-gambit-review/` and `grader-gambit-review/`. The primary now unmounts transient Map stations on successful load/import and cancels Gambit's reveal timer on unmount; the result screen offers another round until five have actually completed. Four Gambit browser tests now pass, including the exact two regressions. At exact fingerprint `0F5D7B36…`, both reviewers independently accepted those original browser reproductions as fixed: the saved 700d/round-zero game persisted beyond the reveal timer, and five rounds played directly with no sixth option. Opened screenshots and scripts are in `artifacts/v2/tester-gambit-retest/` and `grader-gambit-retest/`. Neither report is a whole-game signoff.

The cellar preflight `artifacts/v2/rats-preflight/probe.mjs` showed the old `TAVERN_RATS_RESULT` accepted a fabricated million-denarii reward and negative million-food loss twice. The new `TAVERN_RATS_FINISH` ignores caller-supplied payout fields, derives a bounded rating and food loss from the seeded schedule and catch/escape counts, enforces once per season, and rejects stale seeds; five unit tests cover exact examples, the forgery, invalid actions, and present Gambit/cellar save counters. The original commit was served from an ignored archive on port 5183 for controlled intro/result captures in `artifacts/v2/baseline-rats/`; it was not edited, and the temporary server was stopped. Candidate intro captures at 1366×768 and 390×844 plus result captures at 1366×768, 1280×720, 1920×1080, and 390×844 in `artifacts/v2/rats-browser/` were opened and compared. Instructions and result values are more legible, the result action is visible on phone without horizontal overflow, and the baseline's phone result stacked three stats vertically. At frozen fingerprint `93A2F78E…`, the tester found that overlapping scheduled rats erased five escapes from a normal seed-1 run, and the grader found invisible keyboard focus on active grid cells. Both independently reproduced a bounded direct-action finish claiming 15 catches without play; the tester ranked it P2 and the grader P3. The primary settled replaced rats, accounted for all 15 planned spawns at time-up, and restored the global focus outline. The full focused run, 49 units, typecheck, lint, and build passed. At exact fingerprint `DB0268FC…`, the tester independently repeated the real 390×844 20-second run: one catch and 14 escapes, 28 food charged, saved 452 food and 322 grain, loaded seasonal lockout, no page/console errors. The grader verified a visible solid 3px gold focus ring and opened the full-size mobile result; no new P1/P2 in inspected paths. Reports: `artifacts/v2/tester-rats-retest/` and `grader-rats-retest/`. Catch counts remain self-reported by this client-only game; bounded, deterministic reducer scoring does not attest human input or prevent direct action fabrication.

The stranger trade preflight showed the old `TAVERN_STRANGER_TRADE` accepted `cost: -1_000_000` and `foodReward: 1_000_000`, taking an Easy start from 700d/480 food to 1,000,700d/1,000,480 food without an encounter. At seed 2, three new units and two browser flows pass, including pending offer reload, canonical trade, completed reload, and decline. Because visiting now consumes a saved encounter draw, the previous seed-1 Gambit/Rats browser expectations changed: two wagers are defeats (650d, RNG `3975261312`) and the one-catch cellar run plans 16 rats (15 escapes, 30 food lost, RNG `3087660854`). Reports: `artifacts/v2/tester-stranger-review/`, `grader-stranger-review/`, and matching `*-stranger-retest/` directories.

The Bard preflight reproduced an unlimited +10d payout from the old `TAVERN_BARD_RIDDLE_SOLVED` action. Market preflight: `HAGGLE_START` accepted invented and mismatched merchants; `HAGGLE_COUNTER` accepted a counter as large as `1e308`; the save boundary admitted forged pending offers and `1e308` seasonal prices. The validator checks plausibility of prices and pending terms, not cryptographic authenticity of client-owned saves; other root state fields still require deeper validation.

Mechanics still match `artifacts/v2/raid-engine-preflight/scenarios.json` after excluding the two intentional narrative fields. Opened baseline and candidate images cover title and nine tabs at 1366×768 and 390×844; actual seasonal choices were captured only on the candidate because the baseline capture landed during transition. `artifacts/v2/candidate-ui-3/` adds inspected 1280×720 and 1920×1080 title/Estate/seasonal views and targeted Hall/Chapel/Forge captures, plus a 390×844 phase-scroll before/after image. The initial seasonal screenshots in that folder were dim transition frames; they were recaptured after the Chronicle state settled, and only the latter support visual observations. Full deep interaction, outcome, keyboard/focus, browser zoom, and approved Windows golden coverage remain open. Screenshots are qualitative layout evidence, not proof of gameplay correctness.

## Code census and open gates

Count physical UTF-8 lines, including comments and blanks, from baseline Git blobs and current tracked/untracked first-party files; exclude dependencies, generated output, and ignored artifacts. Source categories: implementation `src/**/*.{js,jsx,ts,tsx}` excluding `src/data`; data `src/data` code files; styles `src/*.css`; executable tests `tests/**/*.{js,jsx,ts,tsx}`; tooling the five selected config/playtest scripts; documentation all `.md`; other text `.json`, `.html`, `.yml`, `.yaml`, `.txt`, `.css`, `.svg` outside preceding categories. Generated tracked QA JSON is included on both sides after restoring it. Type-only modules: zero on both sides; mixed TS implementation counts as implementation.

| Category | Baseline | Candidate |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Implementation | 47 files / 30,642 lines | 59 files / 31,586 lines |
| Authored data | 20 / 13,566 | 21 / 13,849 |
| Styles | 1 / 1,139 | 1 / 1,164 |
| Executable tests | 17 / 3,585 | 52 / 6,909 |
| Selected tooling | 5 / 1,879 | 5 / 1,893 |
| Documentation | 9 / 3,089 | 14 / 3,289 lines |
| Other text | 6 / 8,382 | 9 / 9,455 |
| Inclusive text subtotal | 62,282 | 68,145 |

Production implementation plus authored data and styles is 45,347 baseline versus 46,599 candidate, an increase of 1,252 lines. Implementation alone increased 944. The required measurable total production reduction has **not** been achieved. BlacksmithTab, MapTab, Watchtower, and other large views remain. No content IDs were removed in the current diff, but complete reference validation is still open. First-party JS/JSX migration, complete nested save contracts, full visual/interaction review, and an independent final verdict on the completed design are not done. Six genuine browser campaigns (five turn-40 victories and one Hard famine loss/restart) passed in the prior 105-case run, which had one unrelated blank-load timeout; no full-suite pass is claimed.

Synergy preflight: the archived seven-path/21-tier JavaScript data and evaluator are in the source worktree's ignored `artifacts/v2/synergy-ts-preflight/`, with SHA-256 `B6C4B897…` and `E437F608…`. Before behavioral corrections, `compare.mjs` matched all four registry exports and 1,000 generated states across 21,000 tier-condition calls and 9,000 helper calls. Both reviewers confirmed conversion parity at `7FB3B66B…` and reproduced inherited rule, reward, notification, and saved-tier defects. The converted build passed typecheck/lint/build, 92 units, and independent 14-case Estate/Market browser runs. A redundant six-campaign run was interrupted before any terminal result and is not counted as a pass. Subsequent corrections are in this checkpoint and passed typecheck plus 95 unit tests in the source worktree; corrected-build browser and independent retests remain pending. See `checkpoint.md` for current repository verification.
